- **Total source files reviewed recursively:** **`24`** (`19` previously processed + `5` newly added on `2026-11-28`, including `1` exact SHA-256 duplicate `Powerwear-Industry-Product-Catalogue-2026 (1).pdf` reconciled with zero duplicate products)
- **Raw source model/SKU rows mapped:** **`3,171`**
- **Total deduplicated unique catalog products & series:** **`1,436`** across **7 categories**

- **Master Catalogue PDF generated:** `1` (`Ali-Arshad-Product-Sourcing-Catalogue.pdf`)
- **Individual branded product PDFs generated:** **`1,436`**
- **Privacy & Redaction Status:** All supplier names, factory addresses, emails, phone/WhatsApp/WeChat numbers, supplier social links, and private wholesale/RMB/USD prices were scrubbed and verified **0 leaks** across all public JSON and generated PDFs.

## 3. Incomplete, Visual-Only, or Reconciled Sources

- **Exact Duplicate File Reconciled:** `Powerwear-Industry-Product-Catalogue-2026 (1).pdf` matches `Powerwear-Industry-Product-Catalogue-2026.pdf` byte-for-byte (SHA-256 `14d2b42adbe2e848`) and was deduplicated with zero duplicate pages created.
- **Visual-Only / Rasterized Catalogs (OCR-Extracted):** `LED Track Lights.pdf`, `LED Downlights.pdf`, `Luxmogan Catalogue-2026.pdf`, `Bracelet Catalog Linkyi Jewelry-0316.pdf` (1 stitched sheet), `Tennis necklace and tennis bracelet.pdf` (18 raster pages), and `women's necklace.pdf` (67 raster pages) had zero embedded text layer. All SKUs were extracted via Windows WinRT OCR with deterministic OCR normalization (`80xxx`→`B0xxx`, `NOOOI`→`N0001`, `BOOI1`→`B0011`), and missing technical specs (dimensions, gram weights, plating thickness, GSM weights, MOQs, pricing) are explicitly labeled as confirmed fresh on request.
